Just... tired and slow, like I said. Even when I'm at work and I'm smiling at my patients, inside I just feel like I'm barely holding myself together.\n\nDoctor: How many weeks exactly have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: It's been about three weeks now. It started... well, shortly after I found out I was pregnant.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any decrease in interest or pleasure in activities you normally enjoy since you've been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. I've been ignoring texts from people, too, because... I don't know, none of it really feels worth the effort anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your energy level or how you've been moving since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. It's like... everything I do just feels slowed down. Honestly, it feels like I'm moving through syrup. Even simple things at the clinic, like gathering wound supplies or finishing up my charting, they take me noticeably longer than they should.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Well, that's actually what scared me enough to say something at my prenatal appointment. I've been having these thoughts... like I just wish I wouldn't wake up. I don't have a plan to hurt myself or anything like that, but the thoughts are there, and they really frightened me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty about anything in particular since you've been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I haven't really felt guilty about anything in particular. It's... mostly just feeling so slowed down and having those scary thoughts I mentioned. It's actually kind of the opposite. Like I was saying earlier, I just feel really slowed down. Like I've been saying, I'm just completely slowed down, so I don't have any urge to do that.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your feelings of sadness are present most of the day or nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it's... it's definitely most of the day. I've been having these thoughts... like I just wish I wouldn't wake up.
